# Skills · P · part 36

831 entries, political-scientist-analyst to poq-rebuttal. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **political-scientist-analyst**: Analyzes events through political science lens using IR theory (Realism, Liberalism, Constructivism), comparative politics, institutional analysis, and power dynamics. Provides insights on governance, security, regime change, international cooperation, and policy outcomes. Use when: Political events, international crises, elections, regime transitions, policy changes, conflicts. Evaluates: Power distributions, institutional effects, actor interests, strategic interactions, norms.
